# StereoType Fighter V7: Art Prompts

Generated by `tools/build-v7-prompts.py`. Edit the data in that script and re-run it to regenerate this file, `prompts-v7.json` (one entry per frame, for batch tools) and `manifest.v7.template.json`.

Frame counts here supersede the suggestions in `ART_SPEC.md`.\n\n**V7 visual approval (2026-11-10):** Gaz/Preston/Tyler clothing and identity were revised to match the ChatGPT-generated arcade-realistic draft sheets; see `ART_SPEC.md` for the exact reference filenames. Treat legacy action prop beats in this auto-generated file as provisional visual staging, not permission to revert wardrobe or update combat logic. The data generator has been updated for the three costume locks. Re-run generator when the source poses are formally updated.

## Workflow (do it in this order)

1. **Reference sheet** for each fighter (prompt below). Pick one result and keep it. Use it as the image reference for everything else.
2. **Portrait** for each fighter.
3. **Animation strips**: one prompt per animation (or the single-frame prompts in the JSON if your tool does better one image at a time). Always attach the reference sheet and reuse the same seed where your tool allows.
4. **Slice** strips into numbered frames: `python3 tools/slice-v7-strip.py strip.png 6 art/<id>/<anim>`.
5. **Cutscenes**: one image per prompt in the cutscene section, with the fighter reference sheets attached.
6. Copy `art/manifest.v7.template.json` to `art/manifest.json`.

**Negative prompt** (if your tool has one): text, logo, watermark, caption, extra fingers, extra limbs, cropped feet, cropped hands, different outfit, different face, cartoon, pixel art, celebrity likeness, blurry, deformed

**Style prefix** used in every prompt: Photorealistic fighting-game character art, cinematic studio quality, believable anatomy and fabric,

**Style suffix** used in every prompt: Soft key light from upper left with a subtle cool rim light, sharp focus, consistent face and costume, plain flat mid-grey background (for easy cut-out), no text, no logo, no watermark, no extra limbs, no cropped limbs, no outfit changes.


---

## 4. Cutscene illustrations (`art/scenes/<name>.png`)

Set `"scenes": true` in `art/manifest.json` (already set in the template). Several story scenes share one image. Pairings without their own face-off or aftermath image use the staged portraits (no art needed).

### `prologue-bogan-tradie.png`

Attach reference sheets: `bogan-tradie`

```
Cinematic photoreal film still, 16:9 wide (1920x1080), shallow depth of field, natural skin and fabric detail. Keep the bottom 28% of the frame visually quiet (floor, sky, shadow or blur) because the dialogue box covers it; keep faces and key action in the top 70%. No text, no logos, no watermark. Mood: comic. Light: dawn, 6:42 a.m., warm low sun. A large Australian hardware-store car park at dawn, a sausage-sizzle stand setting up, a work ute with a ladder rack. Gaz (an original fictional Australian tradesman in his mid-30s, stocky athletic build, weathered friendly face with light stubble, short brown hair under a yellow hard hat, orange hi-vis vest with two yellow reflective bands over a grey work shirt with rolled sleeves, navy cargo work trousers, brown steel-capped boots, worn leather work gloves tucked into his belt) leans on the ute with a takeaway coffee, reading a crumpled tournament flyer and raising one eyebrow, hi-vis catching the sunrise.
```

## Content rules

- Every character is an original fictional person. Do not use real people's names or likenesses in prompts.
- Keep the satire on tropes and behaviours (hi-vis and sandwiches, synergy talk, shaker bottles), affectionate and self-aware.
- Injuries in the post-fight scenes are bruises and torn clothing only: warm, not gory.
